<h2 class="title">Our Culture</h2>
	
<p class="headline">Raising the bar in our industry starts by putting people first</p>

<p>We're in tireless pursuit of raising the bar in construction by practicing our craft with excellence and hospitality. That starts with putting the people who do the work first. </p>			</div>
					<div class="section-column" data-colspan="6" style="--colspan:6">
					
<div  class="card-gallery | gallery">
			<ul  class="grid equal-height" style="--max-item-count: 1">

				<div class="card">
										<div class="card-content">
																		<h2 class="card-title">Our team gets to do their best work alongside people they trust, in an environment that invests in their growth rather than just their output. </h2>											</div>
				</div>
			</li>
						<li class="gallery-item">
				<div class="card">
										<div class="card-content">
																		<h2 class="card-title">This energy carries through to our clients, in open communication, faster problem-solving, and a project that runs the way it was supposed to.</h2>											</div>
				</div>
			</li>

	<section  class="section | align-center" data-theme="white" id="impact">
					<div class="section-column" data-colspan="12" style="--colspan:12">
						
<h2 class="title">Impact</h2>
	
<p class="headline"><strong>Our culture in action</strong></p>
			</div>
					<div class="section-column" data-colspan="4" style="--colspan:4">
					
<figure class="image-block" data-width="">
			
<picture class="image">
			<source height="400" srcset="https://gardner-builders.com/media/pages/culture/5f03229f41-1787613027/culture-impact-wellnessspaces-400x400-crop-q75-sharpen10.webp 400w, https://gardner-builders.com/media/pages/culture/5f03229f41-1787613027/culture-impact-wellnessspaces-800x800-crop-q75-sharpen10.webp 800w, https://gardner-builders.com/media/pages/culture/5f03229f41-1787613027/culture-impact-wellnessspaces-1200x1200-crop-q75-sharpen10.webp 1200w, https://gardner-builders.com/media/pages/culture/5f03229f41-1787613027/culture-impact-wellnessspaces-2400x2400-crop-q75-sharpen10.webp 2400w" type="image/webp" width="400" />
	
	<img alt="Gardner employee in a wellness room" decoding="async" height="400" loading="lazy" src="https://gardner-builders.com/media/pages/culture/5f03229f41-1787613027/culture-impact-wellnessspaces-400x400-crop-q80.jpg" srcset="https://gardner-builders.com/media/pages/culture/5f03229f41-1787613027/culture-impact-wellnessspaces-400x400-crop-q80.jpg 400w, https://gardner-builders.com/media/pages/culture/5f03229f41-1787613027/culture-impact-wellnessspaces-800x800-crop-q80.jpg 800w, https://gardner-builders.com/media/pages/culture/5f03229f41-1787613027/culture-impact-wellnessspaces-1200x1200-crop-q80.jpg 1200w, https://gardner-builders.com/media/pages/culture/5f03229f41-1787613027/culture-impact-wellnessspaces-2400x2400-crop-q80.jpg 2400w" width="400">
</picture>
		
		
</figure>	
<h3 class="">Privacy pods</h3>

<p class="p-small">We pioneered the privacy pod: a dedicated, comfortable space on-site that supports someone's well-being in the middle of a workday. It's a small space with a large purpose. By bringing wellness spaces onto job sites, we're working to make mental health a standard priority on every job site.</p>			</div>
					<div class="section-column" data-colspan="4" style="--colspan:4">
					
<figure class="image-block" data-width="">
			
<picture class="image">
			<source height="400" srcset="https://gardner-builders.com/media/pages/culture/06f6718490-1787613027/culture-impact-scaffold-400x400-crop-q75-sharpen10.webp 400w, https://gardner-builders.com/media/pages/culture/06f6718490-1787613027/culture-impact-scaffold-800x800-crop-q75-sharpen10.webp 800w, https://gardner-builders.com/media/pages/culture/06f6718490-1787613027/culture-impact-scaffold-1200x1200-crop-q75-sharpen10.webp 1200w, https://gardner-builders.com/media/pages/culture/06f6718490-1787613027/culture-impact-scaffold-2400x2400-crop-q75-sharpen10.webp 2400w" type="image/webp" width="400" />
	
	<img alt="Scaffolding ladders on industrial job site. Two workers in PPE towards the top of the ladder" decoding="async" height="400" loading="lazy" src="https://gardner-builders.com/media/pages/culture/06f6718490-1787613027/culture-impact-scaffold-400x400-crop-q80.jpg" srcset="https://gardner-builders.com/media/pages/culture/06f6718490-1787613027/culture-impact-scaffold-400x400-crop-q80.jpg 400w, https://gardner-builders.com/media/pages/culture/06f6718490-1787613027/culture-impact-scaffold-800x800-crop-q80.jpg 800w, https://gardner-builders.com/media/pages/culture/06f6718490-1787613027/culture-impact-scaffold-1200x1200-crop-q80.jpg 1200w, https://gardner-builders.com/media/pages/culture/06f6718490-1787613027/culture-impact-scaffold-2400x2400-crop-q80.jpg 2400w" width="400">
</picture>
		
		
</figure>	
<h3 class="">Scaffolding over ladders</h3>

<p class="p-small">On our ground-up and industrial projects, we don't rely on extension ladders. As a standard, we erect scaffolding stair towers instead, because safety shouldn't be a trade-off for efficiency. It's a choice that protects our team and sets an example for how the work can be done.</p>			</div>
					<div class="section-column" data-colspan="4" style="--colspan:4">
					
<figure class="image-block" data-width="">
			
<picture class="image">
			<source height="400" srcset="https://gardner-builders.com/media/pages/culture/52798fa77f-1787613027/culture-impact-equaltime-400x400-crop-q75-sharpen10.webp 400w, https://gardner-builders.com/media/pages/culture/52798fa77f-1787613027/culture-impact-equaltime-800x800-crop-q75-sharpen10.webp 800w, https://gardner-builders.com/media/pages/culture/52798fa77f-1787613027/culture-impact-equaltime-1200x1200-crop-q75-sharpen10.webp 1200w, https://gardner-builders.com/media/pages/culture/52798fa77f-1787613027/culture-impact-equaltime-2400x2400-crop-q75-sharpen10.webp 2400w" type="image/webp" width="400" />
	
	<img alt="Image of two field workers on site" decoding="async" height="400" loading="lazy" src="https://gardner-builders.com/media/pages/culture/52798fa77f-1787613027/culture-impact-equaltime-400x400-crop-q80.jpg" srcset="https://gardner-builders.com/media/pages/culture/52798fa77f-1787613027/culture-impact-equaltime-400x400-crop-q80.jpg 400w, https://gardner-builders.com/media/pages/culture/52798fa77f-1787613027/culture-impact-equaltime-800x800-crop-q80.jpg 800w, https://gardner-builders.com/media/pages/culture/52798fa77f-1787613027/culture-impact-equaltime-1200x1200-crop-q80.jpg 1200w, https://gardner-builders.com/media/pages/culture/52798fa77f-1787613027/culture-impact-equaltime-2400x2400-crop-q80.jpg 2400w" width="400">
</picture>
		
		
</figure>	
<h3 class="">Equal time off, no exceptions</h3>

<p class="p-small">Our field and office teams are treated the same when it comes to time off. That includes paid federal holidays for our entire team, including our union members. Everyone who builds with us deserves to be honored the same way.</p>			</div>
			</section>

<p class="headline"><strong>Building a diverse workforce, where everyone belongs</strong></p>

We're taking real steps by creating new pathways into the industry and building a culture where everyone has equal access to opportunity, room to collaborate, and leaders who hold themselves accountable to that standard.</p>			</div>

<h2 class="title"><strong>Project Seedling</strong></h2>
	
<p class="headline"><strong>Investing in the future of construction professionals</strong></p>

<p>In recent years, construction has had too few clear pathways in. Many people never learn about the opportunities for a fulfilling, rewarding career in this industry, simply because no one showed them the way.</p>
<p class="p-small">Gardner's initiative, Project Seedling, is how we're changing that. We're committed to using our resources and influence to grow the skilled workforce within construction, starting with the people closest to us. We hire zero-hour apprentices and sponsor them through the union, often in partnership with programs like Lake Street Works, giving people a real way into a skilled trade and a career.</p>			</div>
			</section>
	
	
	<section  class="section | align-center" data-theme="soft-green" id="gardnergives">
					<div class="section-column" data-colspan="6" style="--colspan:6">
						
<h2 class="title">Gardner Gives</h2>
	
<p class="headline"><strong>At our core, Gardner strives to build strong communities</strong></p>

<p>We want to uplift and support the mission of each and every business we work with and appreciate the opportunity those relationships provide when it comes to helping our non-pron¼üt community thrive.</p><p>Here are a few of the organizations where we volunteer our time and dedicate resources.</p>			</div>

	<section  class="section | align-center" data-theme="white">
					<div class="section-column" data-colspan="8" style="--colspan:8">
						
<h2 class="title">Careers at Gardner</h2>
	
<p class="headline"><strong>Be a part of something bigger</strong></p>

<p>We are always looking to grow our team with those who share our values. Working with Gardner Builders is more than a job, it is an opportunity to collectively shape our culture, and influence the future of construction in an environment that is collaborative, optimistic, and relational.</p>			</div>
					<div class="section-column" data-colspan="4" style="--colspan:4">
					
<p class="p-small">Please consider applying if you feel you would be a great contributor to our mission.</p><a href="https://gardner-builders.com/career-opportunities" class="button primary">Careers</a>
			</div>
			</section>
